# Hedgewars delivered data ledger

Data source: https://github.com/apmlabs/webwars/blob/406e53fde3cd811f15ca4cc5ff0909e69f58e276/hedgewars/share/hedgewars/Data/

Every game file is listed below with original and delivered hashes. Virtual font alias shares the same blob range. Editable SVG companions are included in data-source.tgz. Original PNG/Ogg bytes are the preferred sources where upstream supplies no editable counterpart. Licence texts, original notices and credits are preserved in legal/. No invariant sections or cover texts were identified in the project GFDL notice. Full distribution terms apply, including source and attribution conditions.
